## `templates\clients\client_form.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block breadcrumb %}
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-6">
    <h6 class="font-semibold mb-0 dark:text-white">
        {% if object %}Editar Cliente{% else %}Registro de Nuevo Cliente{% endif %}
    </h6>
    <ul class="flex items-center gap-[6px]">
        <li class="font-medium">
            <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}" class="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:users-group-two-rounded-broken" class="icon text-lg"></iconify-icon>
                Clientes
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="dark:text-white">-</li>
        <li class="font-medium dark:text-white">Formulario</li>
    </ul>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block content %}

<div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-5">
    <div class="col-span-12">
        <div class="card border-0">
            <div class="card-header">
                <h5 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-0">Datos del cliente</h5>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
                <form method="POST" class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-4 needs-validation" novalidate>
                    {% csrf_token %}

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Razón Social / Empresa</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="solar:home-smile-angle-outline"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" 
                                name="{{ form.name.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.name.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.name.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="Ej: Agencia SEO S.L."
                                value="{{ form.name.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.name.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.name.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.name.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Persona de Contacto</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="f7:person"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" 
                                name="{{ form.contact_name.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.contact_name.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.contact_name.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="Nombre del responsable"
                                value="{{ form.contact_name.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.contact_name.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.contact_name.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.contact_name.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Email de Facturación</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="mage:email"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="email" 
                                name="{{ form.email.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.email.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.email.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="cliente@empresa.com"
                                value="{{ form.email.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.email.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.email.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.email.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Teléfono de Contacto</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="solar:phone-calling-linear"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" 
                                name="{{ form.phone.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.phone.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.phone.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="+34 600 000 000"
                                value="{{ form.phone.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.phone.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.phone.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.phone.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 mt-4">
                        <div class="bg-neutral-50 dark:bg-neutral-700 p-6 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 shadow-sm">
                            <div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-center">
                                
                                <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                                    <label class="form-label font-bold text-primary-600 italic mb-2 block">
                                        1. Presupuesto Mensual
                                    </label>
                                    <div class="flex">
                                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-3 border rounded-e-0 border-e-0 rounded-s-md border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 bg-white text-primary-600 font-bold">
                                            $
                                        </span>
                                        <input type="number" 
                                            step="0.01"
                                            name="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.html_name }}" 
                                            id="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.id_for_label }}" 
                                            class="form-control grow rounded-ss-none rounded-es-none {% if form.authorized_monthly_budget.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                            value="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.value|stringformat:".2f"|default:"0.00" }}"
                                            {% if form.authorized_monthly_budget.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                                        >
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-[10px] mt-2 mb-0 text-neutral-400">Límite de gasto para APIs de pago.</p>
                                </div>

                                <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                                    <label class="form-label font-bold text-secondary-light mb-2 block text-center md:text-left">
                                        2. Estado del Cliente
                                    </label>
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-start gap-4 h-full py-2">
                                        <div class="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-7">
                                            <label class="inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                                <input type="checkbox" 
                                                    name="{{ form.is_active.html_name }}" 
                                                    id="{{ form.is_active.id_for_label }}"
                                                    class="sr-only peer" 
                                                    {% if form.is_active.value %}checked{% endif %}
                                                >
                                                <span class="relative w-11 h-6 bg-gray-400 peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer dark:bg-gray-500 peer-checked:after:translate-x-full rtl:peer-checked:after:-translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:start-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-gray-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all dark:border-gray-600 peer-checked:bg-primary-600"></span>
                                                <span class="line-height-1 font-medium ms-3 peer-checked:text-primary-600 text-md text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">
                                                    Activo
                                                </span>
                                            </label>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-[10px] mt-2 mb-0 text-neutral-400">Si se apaga, no se consumirán créditos.</p>
                                </div>

                            </div> {% if form.authorized_monthly_budget.errors or form.is_active.errors %}
                                <div class="mt-3 p-2 bg-danger-50 text-danger-600 text-xs rounded border border-danger-100">
                                    Corrija los errores en la configuración de la Zona Segura.
                                </div>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 flex items-center justify-end gap-3 mt-6">
                        <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}" class="btn btn-outline-neutral-600 px-6 py-3 radius-8">Cancelar</a>
                        <button class="btn btn-primary-600 px-6 py-3 radius-8 flex items-center gap-2" type="submit">
                            <iconify-icon icon="solar:diskette-bold-duotone" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon>
                            {% if object %}Actualizar Billetera{% else %}Crear Cliente{% endif %}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    // Script de validación extraído del modelo WOWDASH
    (() => {
        'use strict'
        const forms = document.querySelectorAll('.needs-validation')
        Array.from(forms).forEach(form => {
            form.addEventListener('submit', event => {
                if (!form.checkValidity()) {
                    event.preventDefault()
                    event.stopPropagation()
                }
                form.classList.add('was-validated')
            }, false)
        })
    })()
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\clients\client_list.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% load humanize %}
{% block breadcrumb %}
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-6">
    <h6 class="font-semibold mb-0 dark:text-white">
        {% if object %}List View{% else %}Listado de Clientes{% endif %}
    </h6>
    <ul class="flex items-center gap-[6px]">
        <li class="font-medium">
            <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}" class="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:users-group-two-rounded-broken" class="icon text-lg"></iconify-icon>
                Clientes
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="dark:text-white">-</li>
        <li class="font-medium dark:text-white">Listado</li>
    </ul>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block content %}
<div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6">
    <div class="col-span-12">
        <div class="card h-full p-0 rounded-xl border-0 overflow-hidden">
            <div class="card-header border-b border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 bg-white dark:bg-neutral-700 py-4 px-6 flex items-center flex-wrap gap-3 justify-between">
                <div class="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-3">
                    <h6 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-0">Mis Clientes (Billeteras)</h6>
                </div>
                <a href="#" class="btn btn-primary-600 px-4 py-2 radius-8 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <iconify-icon icon="ic:baseline-plus" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon>
                    Nuevo Cliente
                </a>
            </div>
            
            <div class="card-body p-6">
                <div class="table-responsive">
                    <table class="table basic-table border-0 mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th class="bg-neutral-100 dark:bg-neutral-600">ID / Nombre</th>
                                <th class="bg-neutral-100 dark:bg-neutral-600 text-center">Presupuesto Mensual</th>
                                <th class="bg-neutral-100 dark:bg-neutral-600 text-center">Estado</th>
                                <th class="bg-neutral-100 dark:bg-neutral-600 text-end">Acciones</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            {% for client in clients %}
                            <tr>
                                <td>
                                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                                        <div class="flex flex-col">
                                            <span class="text-md font-semibold text-secondary-light">{{ client.name }}</span>
                                            <span class="text-xs text-neutral-400">UUID: {{ client.id|truncatechars:8 }}</span>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                                <td class="text-center">
                                    <span class="text-md font-medium text-secondary-light">
                                        ${{ client.authorized_monthly_budget|default:"0.00" }}
                                    </span>
                                </td>
                                <td class="text-center">
                                    {% if client.is_active %}
                                    <span class="bg-success-focus text-success-main px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs fw-medium">Activo</span>
                                    {% else %}
                                    <span class="bg-danger-focus text-danger-main px-3 py-1 rounded-full text-xs fw-medium">Bloqueado</span>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </td>
                                <td class="text-end">
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-end gap-2">
                                        <a href="{% url 'clients:update' client.pk %}" class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center bg-info-focus text-info-main rounded-lg">
                                            <iconify-icon icon="lucide:edit"></iconify-icon>
                                        </a>
                                        <a href="#" class="w-8 h-8 flex items-center justify-center bg-danger-focus text-danger-main rounded-lg remove-item-btn">
                                            <iconify-icon icon="lucide:trash-2"></iconify-icon>
                                        </a>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            {% empty %}
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="4" class="text-center py-10 text-neutral-400">
                                    No tienes clientes registrados todavía.
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>

                {% if is_paginated %}
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2 mt-6">
                    <span class="text-sm text-neutral-500">Mostrando {{ page_obj.start_index }} a {{ page_obj.end_index }}</span>
                    <ul class="pagination flex items-center gap-2">
                        {% if page_obj.has_previous %}
                            <li><a href="?page={{ page_obj.previous_page_number }}" class="page-link bg-neutral-200 p-2 rounded-lg">Anterior</a></li>
                        {% endif %}
                        <li><span class="text-primary-600 font-bold px-3">{{ page_obj.number }}</span></li>
                        {% if page_obj.has_next %}
                            <li><a href="?page={{ page_obj.next_page_number }}" class="page-link bg-neutral-200 p-2 rounded-lg">Siguiente</a></li>
                        {% endif %}
                    </ul>
                </div>
                {% endif %}
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

---

## `templates\confirm_delete.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row justify-content-center mt-5">
        <div class="col-lg-6">
            <div class="card border-danger shadow-lg">
                <div class="card-header bg-danger text-white py-3">
                    <h4 class="card-title mb-0">
                        <i class="mdi mdi-alert-decagram me-2"></i>Confirmar Eliminación
                    </h4>
                </div>
                <div class="card-body p-4 text-center">
                    <div class="mb-4">
                        <i class="mdi mdi-trash-can-outline text-danger" style="font-size: 4rem;"></i>
                    </div>
                    
                    <h3 class="text-dark">¿Estás seguro?</h3>
                    <p class="text-muted fs-5">
                        Estás a punto de eliminar permanentemente:
                    </p>
                    
                    <div class="alert alert-secondary border-dashed py-3">
                        <span class="fw-bold fs-4 text-dark">{{ object }}</span>
                        {% if object.domain %}<br><small class="text-muted">{{ object.domain }}</small>{% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <p class="text-danger small fw-bold">
                        <i class="mdi mdi-information me-1"></i>
                        Esta acción no se puede deshacer y borrará todos los datos vinculados.
                    </p>

                    <form method="post">
                        {% csrf_token %}
                        <div class="d-flex justify-content-center gap-3 mt-4">
                            <a href="{{ view.get_success_url }}" class="btn btn-light btn-lg px-4">
                                No, cancelar
                            </a>
                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-danger btn-lg px-4">
                                <i class="fe-trash-2 me-1"></i> Sí, eliminar
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\includes\head.html`

```html
{% load static %}

<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
<title>{% block title %}SEOSuite 2026{% endblock %}</title>

    <script src="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/js/lib/iconify-icon.min.js' %}"></script>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:ital,opsz,wght@0,14..32,100..900;1,14..32,100..900&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <!-- remix icon font css  -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/remixicon.css' %}">
    <!-- Apex Chart css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/apexcharts.css' %}">
    <!-- Data Table css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/dataTables.min.css' %}">
    <!-- Text Editor css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/editor-katex.min.css' %} ">  
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/editor.atom-one-dark.min.css' %} ">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/editor.quill.snow.css' %} ">
    <!-- Date picker css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/flatpickr.min.css' %} ">
    <!-- Calendar css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/full-calendar.css' %} ">
    <!-- Vector Map css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href= " {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/jquery-jvectormap-2.0.5.css' %}">
    <!-- Popup css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/magnific-popup.css' %}">
    <!-- Slick Slider css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/slick.css' %} ">
    <!-- prism css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/prism.css' %}">
    <!-- file upload css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/file-upload.css' %}">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/lib/audioplayer.css' %}">
    <!-- main css -->
    <link rel="stylesheet" href=" {% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/style.css'%}">

    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/remixicon.css' %}">
    <link rel="stylesheet" href="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/css/style.css' %}">
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/tom-select@2.3.1/dist/css/tom-select.bootstrap5.min.css" rel="stylesheet">
    {{ form.media.css }}
    
  <style>
    /* 1. Forzar el ancho completo del contenedor */
    .ts-wrapper {
        width: 100% !important;
        display: block !important;
    }

    /* 2. Estilizar el control para que parezca un input de WOWDASH */
    .ts-control {
        width: 100% !important;
        min-height: 48px !important; /* Altura estándar de tus otros inputs */
        padding: 10px 12px 10px 45px !important; /* 45px a la izquierda para el icono */
        border: 1px solid #d1d5db !important; /* gray-300 */
        border-radius: 8px !important;
        background-color: #ffffff !important;
        font-size: 14px !important;
        display: flex !important;
        align-items: center !important;
        transition: border-color 0.2s, box-shadow 0.2s;
    }

    /* 3. Comportamiento del foco */
    .ts-wrapper.focus .ts-control {
        border-color: #4f46e5 !important; /* primary-600 */
        box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(79, 70, 229, 0.1) !important;
    }

    /* 4. Arreglar el icono dentro del campo */
    .icon-field {
        position: relative;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        width: 100%;
    }

    .icon-field .icon {
        position: absolute;
        left: 15px;
        z-index: 10;
        pointer-events: none;
        display: flex;
        align-items: center;
        color: #94a3b8; /* slate-400 */
    }

    /* 5. Asegurar que el dropdown flote sobre todo */
    .ts-dropdown {
        z-index: 9999 !important;
        border-radius: 8px !important;
        margin-top: 5px !important;
        box-shadow: 0 10px 15px -3px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1) !important;
    }
</style>
{% block extra_css %}{% endblock %}
</head>
```

## `templates\includes\navbar.html`

```html


{% load static %}

<div class="navbar-header border-b border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600">
    <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
        <div class="col-auto">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-[16px]">
                <button type="button" class="sidebar-toggle">
                    <iconify-icon icon="heroicons:bars-3-solid" class="icon non-active"></iconify-icon>
                    <iconify-icon icon="iconoir:arrow-right" class="icon active"></iconify-icon>
                </button>
                <button type="button" class="sidebar-mobile-toggle d-flex !leading-[0]">
                    <iconify-icon icon="heroicons:bars-3-solid" class="icon !text-[30px]"></iconify-icon>
                </button>
                <form class="navbar-search">
                    <input type="text" name="search" placeholder="Search">
                    <iconify-icon icon="ion:search-outline" class="icon"></iconify-icon>
                </form>

            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-auto">
            <div class="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
                <button type="button" id="theme-toggle" class="w-10 h-10 bg-neutral-200 dark:bg-neutral-700 dark:text-white rounded-full flex justify-center items-center">
                    <span id="theme-toggle-dark-icon" class="hidden">
                        <i class="ri-sun-line"></i>
                    </span>
                    <span id="theme-toggle-light-icon" class="hidden">
                        <i class="ri-moon-line"></i>
                    </span>
                </button>

                <!-- Language Dropdown Start  -->
                <div class="hidden sm:inline-block">
                    <button data-dropdown-toggle="dropdownInformation" class="has-indicator w-10 h-10 bg-neutral-200 dark:bg-neutral-700 dark:text-white rounded-full flex justify-center items-center" type="button">
                        <img src="{% static 'vendor/wowdash/assets/images/lang-flag.png' %}" alt="image" class="w-6 h-6 object-cover rounded-full">
                    </button>
                    <div id="dropdownInformation" class="z-10 hidden bg-white dark:bg-neutral-700 rounded-lg shadow-lg dropdown-menu-sm p-3">
                        <div class="py-3 px-4 rounded-lg bg-primary-50 dark:bg-primary-600/25 mb-4 flex items-center justify-between gap-2">
                            <div>
                                <h6 class="text-lg text-neutral-900 font-semibold mb-0">Choose Your Language</h6>
                            </div>
                        </div>

                        <div class="max-h-[400px] overflow-y-auto scroll-sm pe-2">
                            <div class="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-4">

                                <div class="form-check style-check flex items-center justify-between">
                                    <label class="form-check-label line-height-1 font-medium text-secondary-light" for="english">
```

## `templates\includes\notification_badge_partial.html`

```html
<a class="nav-link dropdown-toggle" 
   data-bs-toggle="dropdown" 
   href="#" 
   role="button" 
   aria-haspopup="false" 
   aria-expanded="false"
   hx-get="{% url 'seo:mark_notifications_as_read' %}" 
   hx-trigger="click"
   hx-swap="none"
   onclick="const badge = this.querySelector('.noti-icon-badge'); if(badge) badge.remove();">
    
    <i data-feather="bell" class="noti-icon"></i>
    
    {% if unread_notifications_count > 0 %}
        <span class="badge bg-danger rounded-circle noti-icon-badge">
            {{ unread_notifications_count }}
        </span>
    {% endif %}
</a>
<div class="dropdown-menu dropdown-menu-end dropdown-lg">
    <div class="dropdown-item noti-title">
        <h5 class="m-0">Notificaciones</h5>
    </div>

    <div class="noti-scroll" style="max-height: 230px; overflow-y: auto;">
        {% for notification in user_notifications %}
            <a href="{% if notification.audit %}{% url 'seo:url_audit' pk=notification.audit.project.pk %}{% else %}javascript:void(0);{% endif %}" 
               class="dropdown-item notify-item border-bottom {% if not notification.is_read %}bg-light{% endif %}">
                <div class="notify-icon bg-primary"><i class="mdi mdi-google"></i></div>
                <p class="notify-details">
                    {{ notification.message }}
                    <small class="text-muted d-block">{{ notification.created_at|timesince }}</small>
                </p>
            </a>
        {% empty %}
            <div class="p-3 text-center">
                <p class="text-muted m-0">No hay notificaciones</p>
            </div>
        {% endfor %}
    </div>
</div>

<script>
    // Esto es seguro porque feather solo reemplaza iconos
    if (typeof feather !== 'undefined') { feather.replace(); }
</script>
```

```html
{% load static %}
<aside class="sidebar">
    <button type="button" class="sidebar-close-btn !mt-4">
        <iconify-icon icon="radix-icons:cross-2"></iconify-icon>
    </button>
    <div>
        <a href="/" class="sidebar-logo">
            <img src="{% static 'assets/images/logo.png' %}" alt="SEOSuite" class="light-logo">
            <img src="{% static 'assets/images/logo-light.png' %}" alt="SEOSuite" class="dark-logo">
            <img src="{% static 'assets/images/logo-icon.png' %}" alt="SEOSuite" class="logo-icon">
        </a>
    </div>
    <div class="sidebar-menu-area">
        <ul class="sidebar-menu" id="sidebar-menu">
            
            <li class="{% if request.resolver_match.url_name == 'dashboard' %}active{% endif %}">
                <a href="/">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:home-smile-angle-outline" class="menu-icon"></iconify-icon>
                    <span>Dashboard</span>
                </a>
            </li>

            <li class="sidebar-menu-group-title">Applications</li>
            
            <li class="{% if 'clients' in request.path %}active{% endif %}">
                <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:users-group-two-rounded-broken" class="menu-icon"></iconify-icon>
                    <span>Clients (Billeteras)</span>

            </li>

            <li class="{% if 'projects' in request.path %}active{% endif %}">
                <a href="{% url 'projects:list' %}">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:globus-outline" class="menu-icon"></iconify-icon>
                    <span>Projects (Dominios)</span>
                </a>
            </li>

            <li class="sidebar-menu-group-title">SEO Engine</li>

            <li class="dropdown {% if 'keyword_research' in request.path %}open{% endif %}">
                <a href="javascript:void(0)">

                    <span>Keyword Research</span>
                </a>
                <ul class="sidebar-submenu">
                    <li>
                        <a href="{% url 'keyword_research:magic_tool_home' %}" class="{% if request.resolver_match.url_name == 'magic_tool_home' %}text-primary-600{% endif %}">
                            <i class="ri-circle-fill circle-icon text-primary-600 w-auto"></i> Magic Tool
                        </a>
                    </li>
                    <li>

            <li class="sidebar-menu-group-title">Settings</li>

                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:settings-minimalistic-outline" class="menu-icon"></iconify-icon>
                    <span>API Integrations</span>

        </ul>
    </div>
</aside>
```

```html
{% load static %}



<div class="navbar-header">
    <div class="row align-items-center justify-content-between">
        <div class="col-auto">
            <div class="d-flex flex-wrap align-items-center gap-4">
                <button type="button" class="sidebar-toggle">
                    <iconify-icon icon="heroicons:bars-3-solid" class="icon text-2xl non-active"></iconify-icon>
                    <iconify-icon icon="heroicons:x-mark-solid" class="icon text-2xl active"></iconify-icon>
                </button>
                <h6 class="mb-0">{% if active_project %}{{ active_project.name }}{% else %}Selecciona un Proyecto{% endif %}</h6>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="col-auto">
            <div class="d-flex flex-wrap align-items-center gap-3">
                
                {% include 'includes/notification_badge_partial.html' %}

                <div class="dropdown">
                    <button class="d-flex justify-content-center align-items-center rounded-circle" type="button" data-bs-toggle="dropdown">
                        <img src="{{ STATIC_URL }}images/user-avatar.png" alt="image" class="w-40-px h-40-px object-fit-cover rounded-circle">
                    </button>
                    <div class="dropdown-menu to-top dropdown-menu-sm">

                            <h6 class="text-md fw-semibold mb-0">{{ request.user.username }}</h6>

                                <span class="text-xs text-secondary-light">Budget:</span>
                                <span class="text-xs fw-bold {% if budget_alert %}text-danger{% else %}text-success{% endif %}">

                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
```

## `templates\integrations\credential_confirm_delete.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row justify-content-center mt-5">
        <div class="col-md-5">
            <div class="card border-danger border">
                <div class="card-body text-center p-5">
                    <div class="display-4 text-danger mb-3">
                        <i class="mdi mdi-alert-octagon-outline"></i>
                    </div>
                    <h3 class="mb-3">¿Estás seguro?</h3>
                    <p class="text-muted mb-4">
                        Estás a punto de eliminar la credencial <strong>"{{ object.name }}"</strong>. 
                        <br>
                        Esto desconectará cualquier proyecto que dependa de ella para GSC o GA4.
                    </p>

                    <form method="post">
                        {% csrf_token %}
                        <div class="d-grid gap-2 d-md-block">
                            <a href="{% url 'integrations:credential_list' %}" class="btn btn-light px-4">Cancelar</a>
                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-danger px-4">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-delete-forever me-1"></i> Sí, eliminar
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\integrations\credential_form.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row justify-content-center mt-4">
        <div class="col-lg-6">
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <h4 class="mb-3 text-uppercase bg-light p-2">
                        <i class="mdi mdi-key-plus me-1"></i> Nueva Credencial
                    </h4>
                    
                    <div class="alert alert-info border-0 bg-info-lighten text-info">
                        <i class="mdi mdi-information me-1"></i> 
                        Sube el archivo <code>.json</code> que descargaste de Google Cloud Console (IAM & Admin > Service Accounts).
                    </div>

                    {% if form.errors %}
                        <div class="alert alert-danger">{{ form.errors }}</div>
                    {% endif %}

                    <form method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data">
                        {% csrf_token %}
                        
                        <div class="mb-3">
                            <label class="form-label">Cliente Asociado</label>
                            {{ form.client }}
                        </div>

                        <div class="mb-3">
                            <label class="form-label">Nombre para identificarla</label>
                            {{ form.name }}
                        </div>

                        <hr>

                        <div class="mb-3">
                            <label class="form-label fw-bold text-primary">Opción A: Subir Archivo (Recomendado)</label>
                            {{ form.json_file }}
                        </div>

                        <div class="text-center text-muted my-2">- O -</div>

                        <div class="mb-3">
                            <label class="form-label">Opción B: Pegar código manualmente</label>
                            {{ form.service_account_json }}
                        </div>

                        <div class="d-grid gap-2 d-md-flex justify-content-md-end">
                            <button type="button" class="btn btn-light me-2" onclick="window.close()">Cancelar / Cerrar</button>
                            
                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-success px-4">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-cloud-upload me-1"></i> Guardar Credencial
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </form>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\integrations\credential_list.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}

{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h2>Mis Credenciales de Google</h2>
        <a href="#" class="btn btn-primary">+ Añadir JSON</a>
    </div>
    
    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-body">
            <table class="table">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Nombre</th>
                        <th>Email de Servicio</th>
                        <th>Proyectos Asociados</th>
                        <th>Acciones</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    {% for cred in credentials %}
                    <tr>
                        <td>{{ cred.name }}</td>
                        <td>{{ cred.client_email }}</td>
                        <td>{{ cred.project_set.count }}</td>
                        <td>
                            <a href="#" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary">Editar</a>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% empty %}
                    <tr>
                        <td colspan="4" class="text-center">No hay credenciales cargadas.</td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\keyword_research\keyword_magic.html`

```html
<!--templates/keyword_research/keyword_magic.html -->
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% load humanize %}

{% block content %}

        <div class="container-fluid">

            <div class="row">
                <div class="col-12">
                    <div class="page-title-box">
                        <div class="page-title-right">
                            <ol class="breadcrumb m-0">
                                <li class="breadcrumb-item"><a href="{% url 'projects:list' %}">Proyectos</a></li>
                                <li class="breadcrumb-item active">{{ project.name }}</li>
                            </ol>
                        </div>
                        <h4 class="page-title">Magic Tool: <span class="text-primary">{{ run.inputs.seed }}</span></h4>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="row mb-3">
                <div class="col-12">
                    <div class="card mb-3">
                        <div class="card-body py-3">
                            <div class="d-flex flex-wrap align-items-center gap-2 mb-3">
                                <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">
                                    <button class="btn btn-outline-primary active">Todos</button>
                                    <button class="btn btn-outline-primary">Preguntas</button>
                                </div>
                                <div class="btn-group btn-group-sm">
                                    <button class="btn btn-primary">Broad Match</button>
                                    <button class="btn btn-light border">Phrase Match</button>
                                    <button class="btn btn-light border">Exact Match</button>
                                    <a href="{% url 'projects:sync_gsc' project.id %}" class="btn btn-sm btn-light border">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-sync me-1"></i> Sincronizar GSC
                                    </a>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="d-flex flex-wrap gap-2 align-items-center">
                                <div class="input-group input-group-sm w-auto border rounded-pill bg-light px-2 py-1">
                                    <span class="badge bg-soft-primary text-primary rounded-pill me-2">AI-Powered</span>
                                    <input type="text" id="ai-domain" class="form-control border-0 bg-transparent shadow-none" placeholder="Dominio para datos personalizados..." style="min-width: 200px;">
                                    <i class="mdi mdi-auto-fix text-primary ms-2 fs-16"></i>
                                </div>

                                <select id="filter-intent" class="form-select form-select-sm w-auto">
                                    <option value="">Intención</option>
                                    <option value="I">Informativa</option>
                                    <option value="C">Comercial</option>
                                    <option value="T">Transaccional</option>
                                </select>

                                <input type="text" id="include-keywords" class="form-control form-control-sm border w-auto" placeholder="Incluir palabras...">
                                <input type="text" id="exclude-keywords" class="form-control form-control-sm border w-auto" placeholder="Excluir...">
                                
                                <button id="apply-filters" class="btn btn-sm btn-dark px-3">Aplicar</button>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="row">
                <div class="col-md-6 col-xl-4">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="row align-items-center">
                                <div class="col-6">
                                    <div class="avatar-sm bg-soft-primary rounded">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-format-list-bulleted avatar-title font-22 text-primary"></i>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-6 text-end">
                                    <h3 class="text-dark my-1"><span>{{ keywords.count }}</span></h3>
                                    <p class="text-muted mb-0 text-truncate">Total Keywords</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6 col-xl-4">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="row align-items-center">
                                <div class="col-6">
                                    <div class="avatar-sm bg-soft-success rounded">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-trending-up avatar-title font-22 text-success"></i>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-6 text-end">
                                    <h3 class="text-dark my-1"><span>{{ total_volume|intcomma }}</span></h3>
                                    <p class="text-muted mb-0 text-truncate">Volumen Total</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-md-6 col-xl-4">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="row align-items-center">
                                <div class="col-6">
                                    <div class="avatar-sm bg-soft-warning rounded">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-gauge avatar-title font-22 text-warning"></i>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-6 text-end">
                                    <h3 class="text-dark my-1"><span>{{ avg_kd|floatformat:0 }}%</span></h3>
                                    <p class="text-muted mb-0 text-truncate">KD Promedio</p>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="row">
                <div class="col-lg-3">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <h4 class="header-title mb-3">Grupos (Clustering)</h4>
                            <div class="inbox-widget" data-simplebar style="max-height: 500px;">
                                <a href="javascript:void(0);" class="text-reset mb-2 d-block bg-light p-2 rounded fw-bold group-filter-item" data-group="">
                                    <i class="mdi mdi-layers-outline me-1"></i> Todos los resultados
                                    <span class="badge bg-soft-secondary text-secondary float-end">{{ keywords.count }}</span>
                                </a>
                                
                                {% for group in groups %}
                                <div class="inbox-item py-1 border-bottom">
                                    <p class="inbox-item-author mb-0 font-13">
                                        <a href="javascript:void(0);" class="text-dark group-filter-item" data-group="{{ group.name }}">
                                            {{ group.name|capfirst }}
                                            <span class="badge bg-light text-muted float-end">{{ group.count }}</span>
                                        </a>
                                    </p>
                                </div>
                                {% endfor %}
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="col-lg-9">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="table-responsive">
                                <table id="datatable-magic" class="table table-centered table-nowrap mb-0">
                                    <thead class="table-light">
                                        <tr>
                                            <th>Keyword</th>
                                            <th class="text-center">Intent</th>
                                            <th class="text-end">Volumen</th>
                                            <th class="text-center">KD%</th>
                                            <th class="text-end">CPC</th>
                                            <th class="text-center">SERP</th>
                                            <th class="text-center">Acción</th>
                                        </tr>
                                    </thead>
                                    <tbody>
                                        {% for item in keywords %}
                                        <tr>
                                            <td class="fw-bold text-dark">{{ item.keyword }}</td>
                                            
                                            <td class="text-center">
                                                <span class="badge rounded-circle bg-soft-info text-info border border-info" style="width:22px;height:22px;padding:4px 0;">I</span>
                                            </td>

                                            <td class="text-end fw-bold">{{ item.volume|default:0 }}</td>

                                            <td class="text-center">{{ item.competition_level|default:0 }}%</td>

                                            <td class="text-end text-muted">${{ item.cpc|default:0|floatformat:2 }}</td>

                                            <td class="text-center">
                                                {% with duel=item.last_serp_duel %}
                                                    {% if duel %}
                                                        <a href="{% url 'serp:serp_analysis' duel.id %}" class="btn btn-link p-0 text-primary">
                                                            <i class="mdi mdi-text-box-search-outline fs-20"></i>
                                                        </a>
                                                    {% else %}
                                                        <form action="{% url 'serp:run_duel' project.id item.id %}" method="POST" class="m-0">
                                                            {% csrf_token %}
                                                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-xs btn-outline-primary rounded-pill py-0 px-2 fs-10">DUELO</button>
                                                        </form>
                                                    {% endif %}
                                                {% endwith %}
                                            </td>

                                            <td class="text-center">
                                                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-success rounded-pill px-3">Añadir</button>
                                            </td>
                                        </tr>
                                        {% endfor %}
                                    </tbody>
                                </table>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div> 

{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    // Destruimos cualquier rastro previo para evitar errores
    if ($.fn.DataTable.isDataTable('#datatable-magic')) {
        $('#datatable-magic').DataTable().destroy();
    }

    // Inicialización limpia
    $('#datatable-magic').DataTable({
        "language": { "url": "//cdn.datatables.net/plug-ins/1.13.7/i18n/es-ES.json" },
        "order": [[2, "desc"]], // Ordenar por volumen por defecto
        "pageLength": 25,
        "columnDefs": [
            { "orderable": false, "targets": [1, 5, 6] } // Desactivar orden en iconos y botones
        ]
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\keyword_research\keyword_search_home.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block content %}
<link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/select2@4.1.0-rc.0/dist/css/select2.min.css" rel="stylesheet" />
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/select2-bootstrap-5-theme@1.3.0/dist/select2-bootstrap-5-theme.min.css" />

<style>
    .search-card {
        border-radius: 15px;
        box-shadow: 0 10px 25px rgba(0,0,0,0.05);
        border: none;
    }
    .input-group-lg .form-control {
        border-radius: 8px 0 0 8px;
    }
    .input-group-lg .input-group-text {
        border-radius: 8px 0 0 8px;
    }
    .financial-box {
        background: #f8f9fa;
        border-radius: 12px;
        border-left: 5px solid #0ab39c; /* Color verde de éxito */
    }
    .select2-container--bootstrap-5 .select2-selection {
        height: calc(3.5rem + 2px);
        padding: 0.75rem 0.75rem;
        font-size: 1rem;
    }
</style>

<div class="page-content">
    <div class="container-fluid">

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-12">
                <div class="page-title-box d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">
                    <h4 class="mb-0">Keyword Magic Tool</h4>
                    <div class="page-title-right">
                        <ol class="breadcrumb m-0">
                            <li class="breadcrumb-item"><a href="javascript: void(0);">SEO Tools</a></li>
                            <li class="breadcrumb-item active">Keyword Research</li>
                        </ol>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="row justify-content-center mt-4">
            <div class="col-xl-8">
                <div class="card search-card">
                    <div class="card-body p-5">
                        <div class="text-center mb-4">
                            <h3 class="fw-bold">Descubre nuevas oportunidades</h3>
                            <p class="text-muted">Analiza palabras clave semilla para obtener volúmenes de búsqueda, CPC y dificultad real.</p>
                        </div>

                        <form method="POST" action="{% url 'keyword_research:magic_tool_home' %}" id="magic-search-form">
                            {% csrf_token %}
                            
                            <input type="hidden" name="project" value="{{ project.id }}">

                            <div class="mb-4">
                                <label class="form-label fw-bold text-uppercase small">Palabra Clave Semilla</label>
                                <div class="input-group input-group-lg">
                                    <span class="input-group-text bg-light border-end-0"><i class="mdi mdi-magnify text-primary"></i></span>
                                    <input type="text" name="seed" class="form-control border-start-0 shadow-none" 
                                           placeholder="Ej: seguro de auto, comprar iphone..." required>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="row g-4 mb-4">
                                <div class="col-md-6">
                                    <label class="form-label fw-bold text-uppercase small">País / Ubicación</label>
                                    <select name="location_id" id="location_select" class="form-select">
                                        <option value="2840" selected>United States (Country)</option>
                                        <option value="2724">Spain (Country)</option>
                                        <option value="2484">Mexico (Country)</option>
                                        <option value="2170">Colombia (Country)</option>
                                    </select>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-md-6">
                                    <label class="form-label fw-bold text-uppercase small">Idioma</label>
                                    <select name="language_id" id="language_select" class="form-select">
                                        <option value="1003" selected>Spanish</option>
                                        <option value="1000">English</option>
                                    </select>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="financial-box p-3 mb-4">
                                <div class="row align-items-center">
                                    <div class="col-md-7 border-end">
                                        <p class="mb-1 text-muted small fw-bold text-uppercase">Presupuesto del Proyecto: <span class="text-primary">{{ project.name }}</span></p>
                                        <h4 class="mb-0 text-dark fw-bold">${{ project.authorized_monthly_budget|default:"0.00" }} <span class="badge bg-soft-success text-success fs-12 ms-2">Disponible</span></h4>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="col-md-5 ps-4">
                                        <label class="form-label small fw-bold text-uppercase text-muted mb-1">Modo de consulta</label>
                                        <select name="search_mode" class="form-select form-select-sm border-0 bg-transparent fw-bold shadow-none p-0">
                                            <option value="live">⚡ DataForSEO Labs (Live)</option>
                                            <option value="database">📚 Database (Histórico)</option>
                                        </select>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="d-grid">
                                <button type="submit" id="btn-submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-lg fw-bold py-3 shadow">
                                    <i class="mdi mdi-rocket-launch me-1"></i> INICIAR INVESTIGACIÓN PROFESIONAL
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<script src="https://code.jquery.com/jquery-3.7.1.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/select2@4.1.0-rc.0/dist/js/select2.min.js"></script>

<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    // 1. Inicializar Select2 con el tema Bootstrap 5
    $('#location_select, #language_select').select2({
        theme: 'bootstrap-5',
        width: '100%'
    });

    // 2. Autocomplete de Ubicación vía AJAX (Opcional, pero recomendado)
    $('#location_select').select2({
        theme: 'bootstrap-5',
        ajax: {
            url: "{% url 'keyword_research:location_search' %}",
            dataType: 'json',
            delay: 300,
            data: params => ({ q: params.term }),
            processResults: data => ({ results: data.results })
        },
        minimumInputLength: 2,
        placeholder: "🔍 Busca país o ciudad..."
    });

    // 3. Manejo de estado del botón al enviar
    $('#magic-search-form').on('submit', function() {
        const $btn = $('#btn-submit');
        $btn.prop('disabled', true);
        $btn.html('<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span> PROCESANDO DATOS DE API...');
        // El formulario se enviará de forma natural
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

---

## `templates\keyword_research\project_history.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load humanize %}

{% block content %}
        <div class="container-fluid">
            <div class="row">
                <div class="col-12">
                    <div class="page-title-box">
                        <div class="page-title-right">
                            <ol class="breadcrumb m-0">
                                <li class="breadcrumb-item"><a href="{% url 'projects:list' %}">Proyectos</a></li>
                                <li class="breadcrumb-item"><a href="javascript: void(0);">{{ sidebar_project.name }}</a></li>
                                <li class="breadcrumb-item active">Historial Magic Tool</li>
                            </ol>
                        </div>
                        <h4 class="page-title">Historial de Investigaciones</h4>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="row">
                <div class="col-12">
                    <div class="card">
                        <div class="card-body">
                            <div class="d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between mb-4">
                                <h4 class="header-title">Reportes Guardados</h4>
                                <a href="{% url 'keyword_research:magic_tool_home' %}" class="btn btn-sm btn-primary">
                                    <i class="mdi mdi-plus-circle me-1"></i> Nueva Búsqueda
                                </a>
                            </div>

                            <p class="text-muted font-14 mb-4">
                                Consulta los resultados de tus búsquedas previas sin consumir créditos adicionales de la API.
                            </p>

                            <table id="datatable-history" class="table dt-responsive nowrap w-100">
                                <thead class="table-light">
                                    <tr>
                                        <th>Fecha</th>
                                        <th>Seed Keyword</th>
                                        <th>País / Idioma</th>
                                        <th class="text-center">Keywords</th>
                                        <th>Estatus</th>
                                        <th class="text-end">Acción</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>

                                <tbody>
                                    {% for run in runs %}
                                    <tr>
                                        <td class="align-middle">{{ run.created_at|date:"d/m/Y H:i" }}</td>
                                        <td class="align-middle">
                                            <span class="fw-semibold text-dark">{{ run.inputs.seed }}</span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td class="align-middle">
                                            <i class="mdi mdi-map-marker-outline text-muted"></i> {{ run.inputs.location_name|default:run.inputs.location }}
                                            <br>
                                            <small class="text-muted">{{ run.inputs.language_name|default:run.inputs.language }}</small>
                                        </td>
                                        <td class="align-middle text-center">
                                            <span class="badge bg-soft-info text-info p-1 px-2">{{ run.keywordidea_set.count }} ideas</span>
                                        </td>
                                        <td class="align-middle">
                                            {% if run.status == 'SUCCESS' %}
                                                <span class="badge bg-success">Completado</span>
                                            {% else %}
                                                <span class="badge bg-warning">{{ run.status }}</span>
                                            {% endif %}
                                        </td>
                                        <td class="align-middle text-end">
                                            <a href="{% url 'keyword_research:magic_tool' run.id %}" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary waves-effect waves-light">
                                                <i class="mdi mdi-eye-circle-outline me-1"></i> Ver Resultados
                                            </a>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                    {% empty %}
                                    <tr>
                                        <td colspan="6" class="text-center py-5">
                                            <p class="text-muted">No se encontraron búsquedas para este proyecto.</p>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                    {% endfor %}
                                </tbody>
                            </table>
                        </div> </div> </div></div></div> 
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    $(document).ready(function() {
        // Hando usa datatables con esta clase específica para los estilos del tema
        $('#datatable-history').DataTable({
            "language": {
                "url": "//cdn.datatables.net/plug-ins/1.13.7/i18n/es-ES.json"
            },
            "order": [[0, "desc"]],
            "drawCallback": function() {
                $('.dataTables_paginate > .pagination').addClass('pagination-rounded');
            }
        });
    });
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\patterns\form_standard_v1.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block breadcrumb %}
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-6">
    <h6 class="font-semibold mb-0 dark:text-white">
        {% if object %}Editar Cliente{% else %}Registro de Nuevo Cliente{% endif %}
    </h6>
    <ul class="flex items-center gap-[6px]">
        <li class="font-medium">
            <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}" class="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:users-group-two-rounded-broken" class="icon text-lg"></iconify-icon>
                Clientes
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="dark:text-white">-</li>
        <li class="font-medium dark:text-white">Formulario</li>
    </ul>
</div>
{% endblock %}
{% block content %}

<div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-5">
    <div class="col-span-12">
        <div class="card border-0">
            <div class="card-header">
                <h5 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-0">Datos del cliente</h5>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body">
                <form method="POST" class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-4 needs-validation" novalidate>
                    {% csrf_token %}

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Razón Social / Empresa</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="solar:home-smile-angle-outline"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" 
                                name="{{ form.name.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.name.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.name.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="Ej: Agencia SEO S.L."
                                value="{{ form.name.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.name.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.name.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.name.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Persona de Contacto</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="f7:person"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" 
                                name="{{ form.contact_name.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.contact_name.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.contact_name.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="Nombre del responsable"
                                value="{{ form.contact_name.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.contact_name.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.contact_name.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.contact_name.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Email de Facturación</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="mage:email"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="email" 
                                name="{{ form.email.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.email.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.email.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="cliente@empresa.com"
                                value="{{ form.email.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.email.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.email.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.email.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Teléfono de Contacto</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon">
                                <iconify-icon icon="solar:phone-calling-linear"></iconify-icon>
                            </span>
                            <input type="text" 
                                name="{{ form.phone.html_name }}" 
                                id="{{ form.phone.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control {% if form.phone.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                placeholder="+34 600 000 000"
                                value="{{ form.phone.value|default_if_none:"" }}"
                                {% if form.phone.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                            >
                        </div>
                        {% if form.phone.errors %}
                            {% for error in form.phone.errors %}
                                <div class="text-danger-600 text-sm mt-1">{{ error }}</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        {% endif %}
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 mt-4">
                        <div class="bg-neutral-50 dark:bg-neutral-700 p-6 rounded-lg border border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 shadow-sm">
                            <div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-center">
                                
                                <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                                    <label class="form-label font-bold text-primary-600 italic mb-2 block">
                                        1. Presupuesto Mensual
                                    </label>
                                    <div class="flex">
                                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-3 border rounded-e-0 border-e-0 rounded-s-md border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 bg-white text-primary-600 font-bold">
                                            $
                                        </span>
                                        <input type="number" 
                                            step="0.01"
                                            name="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.html_name }}" 
                                            id="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.id_for_label }}" 
                                            class="form-control grow rounded-ss-none rounded-es-none {% if form.authorized_monthly_budget.errors %}is-invalid{% endif %}" 
                                            value="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.value|stringformat:".2f"|default:"0.00" }}"
                                            {% if form.authorized_monthly_budget.field.required %}required{% endif %}
                                        >
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-[10px] mt-2 mb-0 text-neutral-400">Límite de gasto para APIs de pago.</p>
                                </div>

                                <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                                    <label class="form-label font-bold text-secondary-light mb-2 block text-center md:text-left">
                                        2. Estado del Cliente
                                    </label>
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-start gap-4 h-full py-2">
                                        <div class="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-7">
                                            <label class="inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                                <input type="checkbox" 
                                                    name="{{ form.is_active.html_name }}" 
                                                    id="{{ form.is_active.id_for_label }}"
                                                    class="sr-only peer" 
                                                    {% if form.is_active.value %}checked{% endif %}
                                                >
                                                <span class="relative w-11 h-6 bg-gray-400 peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer dark:bg-gray-500 peer-checked:after:translate-x-full rtl:peer-checked:after:-translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:start-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-gray-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all dark:border-gray-600 peer-checked:bg-primary-600"></span>
                                                <span class="line-height-1 font-medium ms-3 peer-checked:text-primary-600 text-md text-gray-600 dark:text-gray-300">
                                                    Activo
                                                </span>
                                            </label>
                                        </div>
                                    </div>
                                    <p class="text-[10px] mt-2 mb-0 text-neutral-400">Si se apaga, no se consumirán créditos.</p>
                                </div>

                            </div> {% if form.authorized_monthly_budget.errors or form.is_active.errors %}
                                <div class="mt-3 p-2 bg-danger-50 text-danger-600 text-xs rounded border border-danger-100">
                                    Corrija los errores en la configuración de la Zona Segura.
                                </div>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 flex items-center justify-end gap-3 mt-6">
                        <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}" class="btn btn-outline-neutral-600 px-6 py-3 radius-8">Cancelar</a>
                        <button class="btn btn-primary-600 px-6 py-3 radius-8 flex items-center gap-2" type="submit">
                            <iconify-icon icon="solar:diskette-bold-duotone" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon>
                            {% if object %}Actualizar Billetera{% else %}Crear Cliente{% endif %}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
    // Script de validación extraído del modelo WOWDASH
    (() => {
        'use strict'
        const forms = document.querySelectorAll('.needs-validation')
        Array.from(forms).forEach(form => {
            form.addEventListener('submit', event => {
                if (!form.checkValidity()) {
                    event.preventDefault()
                    event.stopPropagation()
                }
                form.classList.add('was-validated')
            }, false)
        })
    })()
</script>
{% endblock %}
```
