## `templates\projects\integrations_setup.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}

{% block content %}
<div class="page-content">
    <div class="container-fluid">

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-12">
                <div class="page-title-box d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">
                    <h4 class="mb-0 text-uppercase fw-bold">Configuración de Integraciones</h4>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-xl-8">
                <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-header bg-white py-3 border-bottom">
                        <h5 class="card-title mb-0 text-primary"><i class="mdi mdi-cog-outline me-1"></i> Parámetros del Proyecto</h5>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card-body p-4">
                        <form method="post" id="mainIntegrationForm">
                            {% csrf_token %}
                            
                            <div class="mb-4">
                                <label class="form-label fw-bold text-uppercase small text-muted">Service Account (JSON)</label>
                                <div class="input-group">
                                    {{ form.google_credential }}
                                    <button class="btn btn-outline-success" type="button" onclick="openCredModal('create')" title="Nueva">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-plus"></i>
                                    </button>
                                    <button class="btn btn-outline-primary" type="button" onclick="openCredModal('edit')" title="Editar">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-pencil"></i>
                                    </button>
                                    <button class="btn btn-outline-danger" type="button" onclick="deleteCredential()" title="Eliminar">
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-close"></i>
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                                <div class="form-text mt-2 italic">La llave maestra que otorga acceso a las APIs de Google.</div>
                            </div>

                            <hr class="my-4 border-light">

                            <div class="row g-4">
                                <div class="col-md-12">
                                    <label class="form-label fw-bold small text-muted text-uppercase">G. Search Console (URL)</label>
                                    {{ form.gsc_property_url }}
                                    <div class="form-text">Ej: https://epicmoments.media/ (Debe incluir / al final)</div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-md-6">
                                    <label class="form-label fw-bold small text-muted text-uppercase">Analytics (GA4 Property ID)</label>
                                    {{ form.ga4_property_id }}
                                    <div class="form-text">Ej: 517901959</div>
                                </div>
                                <div class="col-md-6">
                                    <label class="form-label fw-bold small text-muted text-uppercase">Google Ads (Customer ID)</label>
                                    {{ form.google_ads_customer_id }}
                                    <div class="form-text">Ej: 511-099-9249</div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="mt-5 text-end border-top pt-4">
                                <button type="submit" class="btn btn-primary btn-lg px-5 shadow-sm fw-bold">
                                    <i class="mdi mdi-content-save me-1"></i> GUARDAR TODO
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-xl-4">
                <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-header bg-white py-3 border-bottom">
                        <h5 class="card-title mb-0 text-primary">Estado de Conexión</h5>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card-body p-0">
                        <div class="list-group list-group-flush">
                            
                            <div class="list-group-item py-3 px-4">
                                <div class="d-flex align-items-center">
                                    <div class="avatar-sm flex-shrink-0">
                                        <span class="avatar-title {% if object.gsc_verified %}bg-success text-white{% else %}bg-soft-info text-info{% endif %} rounded-circle" id="icon-gsc">
                                            <i class="mdi {% if object.gsc_verified %}mdi-check-bold{% else %}mdi-google-search{% endif %} font-22"></i>
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="flex-grow-1 ms-3">
                                        <h6 class="mb-1 fw-bold">Search Console</h6>
                                        <small id="msg-gsc" class="{% if object.gsc_verified %}text-success fw-bold{% else %}text-muted{% endif %} small">
                                            {% if object.gsc_verified %}Conexión verificada{% else %}Pendiente de verificación{% endif %}
                                        </small>
                                    </div>
                                    <button onclick="runTest('gsc')" class="btn btn-sm {% if object.gsc_verified %}btn-soft-success{% else %}btn-light border{% endif %} shadow-sm" id="btn-gsc">
                                        {% if object.gsc_verified %}RE-TEST{% else %}TEST{% endif %}
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="list-group-item py-3 px-4">
                                <div class="d-flex align-items-center">
                                    <div class="avatar-sm flex-shrink-0">
                                        <span class="avatar-title {% if object.ga4_verified %}bg-success text-white{% else %}bg-soft-success text-success{% endif %} rounded-circle" id="icon-ga4">
                                            <i class="mdi {% if object.ga4_verified %}mdi-check-bold{% else %}mdi-chart-bar{% endif %} font-22"></i>
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="flex-grow-1 ms-3">
                                        <h6 class="mb-1 fw-bold">Analytics GA4</h6>
                                        <small id="msg-ga4" class="{% if object.ga4_verified %}text-success fw-bold{% else %}text-muted{% endif %} small">
                                            {% if object.ga4_verified %}Conexión verificada{% else %}Sin verificar{% endif %}
                                        </small>
                                    </div>
                                    <button onclick="runTest('ga4')" class="btn btn-sm {% if object.ga4_verified %}btn-soft-success{% else %}btn-light border{% endif %} shadow-sm" id="btn-ga4">
                                        {% if object.ga4_verified %}RE-TEST{% else %}TEST{% endif %}
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="list-group-item py-3 px-4">
                                <div class="d-flex align-items-center">
                                    <div class="avatar-sm flex-shrink-0">
                                        <span class="avatar-title {% if object.ads_verified %}bg-success text-white{% else %}bg-soft-warning text-warning{% endif %} rounded-circle" id="icon-ads">
                                            <i class="mdi {% if object.ads_verified %}mdi-check-bold{% else %}mdi-google-ads{% endif %} font-22"></i>
                                        </span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="flex-grow-1 ms-3">
                                        <h6 class="mb-1 fw-bold">Google Ads</h6>
                                        <small id="msg-ads" class="{% if object.ads_verified %}text-success fw-bold{% else %}text-muted{% endif %} small">
                                            {% if object.ads_verified %}Conexión verificada{% else %}Sin verificar{% endif %}
                                        </small>
                                    </div>
                                    <button onclick="runTest('ads')" class="btn btn-sm {% if object.ads_verified %}btn-soft-success{% else %}btn-light border{% endif %} shadow-sm" id="btn-ads">
                                        {% if object.ads_verified %}RE-TEST{% else %}TEST{% endif %}
                                    </button>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<div class="modal fade" id="modalCredential" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">
    <div class="modal-dialog modal-lg modal-dialog-centered">
        <div class="modal-content shadow-lg border-0">
            <div class="modal-header bg-light">
                <h5 class="modal-title fw-bold" id="modalTitle">Credencial de Google</h5>
                <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close"></button>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-body p-4">
                <input type="hidden" id="cred_id">
                <div class="mb-3">
                    <label class="form-label fw-bold text-muted small">Nombre Identificador</label>
                    <input type="text" id="cred_name" class="form-control" placeholder="Ej: SA Proyecto Epic">
                </div>
                <div class="mb-0">
                    <label class="form-label fw-bold text-muted small">Contenido JSON (Service Account)</label>
                    <textarea id="cred_json" class="form-control font-monospace text-primary bg-light" rows="12" style="font-size: 12px;"></textarea>
                </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer bg-light">
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-white" data-bs-dismiss="modal">Cerrar</button>
                <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary px-4 shadow-sm" onclick="saveCredential()">
                    <i class="mdi mdi-check me-1"></i> Guardar Credencial
                </button>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>

<style>
    .avatar-sm { height: 42px; width: 42px; }
    .bg-soft-info { background-color: rgba(59, 175, 218, 0.15) !important; color: #3bafda !important; }
    .bg-soft-success { background-color: rgba(26, 188, 156, 0.15) !important; color: #1abc9c !important; }
    .bg-soft-warning { background-color: rgba(246, 187, 66, 0.15) !important; color: #f6bb42 !important; }
    .font-monospace { font-family: 'Courier New', Courier, monospace !important; }
    .italic { font-style: italic; }
</style>



<script>
const bsModal = new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('modalCredential'));

// GESTIÓN DE CREDENCIALES
function openCredModal(mode) {
    const credSelect = document.getElementById('id_google_credential');
    const credId = credSelect.value;

    if (mode === 'create') {
        document.getElementById('modalTitle').innerText = "Nueva Service Account";
        document.getElementById('cred_id').value = "";
        document.getElementById('cred_name').value = "";
        document.getElementById('cred_json').value = "";
        bsModal.show();
    } else {
        if (!credId) { alert("Selecciona una credencial primero."); return; }
        fetch(`/integrations/credentials/${credId}/get/`)
            .then(res => res.json())
            .then(data => {
                document.getElementById('modalTitle').innerText = "Editar Credencial: " + data.name;
                document.getElementById('cred_id').value = data.id;
                document.getElementById('cred_name').value = data.name;
                document.getElementById('cred_json').value = JSON.stringify(data.json, null, 2);
                bsModal.show();
            });
    }
}

function saveCredential() {
    const id = document.getElementById('cred_id').value;
    const payload = {
        name: document.getElementById('cred_name').value,
        service_account_json: document.getElementById('cred_json').value
    };
    if (!payload.name || !payload.service_account_json) return alert("Completa los campos.");

    const url = id ? `/integrations/credentials/${id}/update/` : "/integrations/credentials/quick-create/";

    fetch(url, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "X-CSRFToken": "{{ csrf_token }}" },
        body: JSON.stringify(payload)
    }).then(res => res.json()).then(data => {
        if (data.status === 'success') location.reload();
        else alert("Error: " + data.message);
    });
}

function deleteCredential() {
    const credId = document.getElementById('id_google_credential').value;
    if (!credId) return alert("Selecciona una credencial.");
    if (confirm("¿Eliminar permanentemente?")) {
        fetch(`/integrations/credentials/${credId}/delete/`, {
            method: "POST",
            headers: { "X-CSRFToken": "{{ csrf_token }}" }
        }).then(() => location.reload());
    }
}

// GESTIÓN DE TESTS
function runTest(apiType) {
    const btn = document.getElementById(`btn-${apiType}`);
    const msg = document.getElementById(`msg-${apiType}`);
    const icon = document.getElementById(`icon-${apiType}`);

    btn.disabled = true;
    btn.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm"></span>';
    msg.innerText = 'Verificando...';

    fetch(`/projects/{{ object.id }}/test-access/?type=${apiType}`)
        .then(res => res.json())
        .then(data => {
            msg.innerText = data.message;
            if (data.status === 'success') {
                msg.className = 'text-success small fw-bold';
                icon.className = 'avatar-title bg-success text-white rounded-circle';
                icon.innerHTML = '<i class="mdi mdi-check-bold font-22"></i>';
                btn.className = 'btn btn-sm btn-soft-success shadow-sm';
                btn.innerText = 'RE-TEST';
            } else {
                msg.className = 'text-danger small fw-bold';
                icon.className = 'avatar-title bg-danger text-white rounded-circle';
                icon.innerHTML = '<i class="mdi mdi-alert-circle font-22"></i>';
            }
        })
        .finally(() => {
            btn.disabled = false;
            if (!btn.innerText.includes('RE-TEST')) btn.innerText = 'TEST';
        });
}
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\projects\project_form.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% load django_tomselect %}
{% block extra_css %}
 
{% endblock %}
{% block breadcrumb %}
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-6">
    <h6 class="font-semibold mb-0 dark:text-white">
        {% if object %}Editar Proyecto: {{ object.name }}{% else %}Registro de Nuevo Dominio{% endif %}
    </h6>
    <ul class="flex items-center gap-[6px]">
        <li class="font-medium">
            <a href="{% url 'projects:list' %}" class="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:globus-outline" class="icon text-lg"></iconify-icon>
                Proyectos
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="dark:text-white">-</li>
        <li class="font-medium dark:text-white">Formulario</li>
    </ul>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
<div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6">
    
    <div class="col-span-12 lg:col-span-8">
        <div class="card border-0 shadow-sm radius-12">
            <div class="card-header border-b border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 p-6">
                <h5 class="text-lg font-semibold mb-0 text-secondary-light">Ficha de Configuración SEO</h5>
            </div>
            <div class="card-body p-6">
                <form method="POST" class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-4 needs-validation" novalidate>
                    {% csrf_token %}

                    {% if request.GET.client or object.client %}
                        <input type="hidden" name="client" value="{% if object %}{{ object.client.id }}{% else %}{{ request.GET.client }}{% endif %}">
                    {% else %}
                        <div class="col-span-12 mb-2">
                            <label class="form-label font-bold text-primary-600">Asignar a Cliente / Billetera *</label>
                            <select name="{{ form.client.html_name }}" id="{{ form.client.id_for_label }}" class="form-control form-select" required>
                                {% for choice in form.client.field.choices %}
                                    <option value="{{ choice.0 }}" {% if form.client.value|stringformat:"s" == choice.0|stringformat:"s" %}selected{% endif %}>{{ choice.1 }}</option>
                                {% endfor %}
                            </select>
                        </div>
                    {% endif %}

                    <div class="col-span-12 mt-4"><h6 class="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-neutral-400 tracking-widest">1. Identificación del Sitio</h6></div>
                    
                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Nombre del Proyecto</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon"><iconify-icon icon="solar:pen-new-square-linear"></iconify-icon></span>
                            <input type="text" name="{{ form.name.html_name }}" id="{{ form.name.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control" placeholder="Ej: Mi E-commerce" 
                                value="{{ form.name.value|default_if_none:"" }}" required>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Dominio / URL Principal</label>
                        <div class="icon-field has-validation">
                            <span class="icon"><iconify-icon icon="solar:globus-linear"></iconify-icon></span>
                            <input type="text" name="{{ form.domain.html_name }}" id="{{ form.domain.id_for_label }}" 
                                class="form-control" placeholder="midominio.com" 
                                value="{{ form.domain.value|default_if_none:"" }}" required>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 border-t border-neutral-100 pt-6 mt-2">
                        <h6 class="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-neutral-400 tracking-widest">2. Geo-Engine (Ubicación de Rastreo)</h6>
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Ubicación Objetivo</label>
                        <div class="icon-field">
                            <span class="icon"><iconify-icon icon="solar:map-point-wave-linear" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon></span>
                            {{ form.location_selector }}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label">Idioma de Búsqueda</label>
                        <div class="icon-field">
                            <span class="icon"><iconify-icon icon="solar:chat-round-line-linear" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon></span>
                            {{ form.language_selector }}
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 border-t border-neutral-100 pt-6 mt-2"><h6 class="text-xs font-bold uppercase text-neutral-400 tracking-widest">3. Integraciones de Datos</h6></div>

                    <div class="col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label text-xs font-bold">Google Credential (Service Account JSON)</label>
                        <div class="flex gap-2">
                            <div class="grow">
                                <select name="{{ form.google_credential.html_name }}" id="{{ form.google_credential.id_for_label }}" class="form-control form-select">
                                    {% for choice in form.google_credential.field.choices %}
                                        <option value="{{ choice.0 }}" {% if form.google_credential.value|stringformat:"s" == choice.0|stringformat:"s" %}selected{% endif %}>{{ choice.1 }}</option>
                                    {% endfor %}
                                </select>
                            </div>
                            <a href="{% url 'integrations:credential_add' %}" target="_blank" class="btn btn-outline-neutral-400 flex items-center gap-1 px-4 hover:bg-neutral-100">
                                <iconify-icon icon="solar:add-circle-linear" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon> Gestionar
                            </a>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-4 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-neutral-400">Search Console URL</label>
                        <input type="text" name="{{ form.gsc_property_url.html_name }}" class="form-control text-sm" value="{{ form.gsc_property_url.value|default_if_none:"" }}">
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-4 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-neutral-400">GA4 Property ID</label>
                        <input type="text" name="{{ form.ga4_property_id.html_name }}" class="form-control text-sm" value="{{ form.ga4_property_id.value|default_if_none:"" }}">
                    </div>

                    <div class="md:col-span-4 col-span-12">
                        <label class="form-label text-[10px] uppercase font-bold text-neutral-400">Ads Customer ID</label>
                        <input type="text" name="{{ form.google_ads_customer_id.html_name }}" class="form-control text-sm" value="{{ form.google_ads_customer_id.value|default_if_none:"" }}">
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 mt-6">
                        <div class="bg-neutral-50 dark:bg-neutral-700 p-6 rounded-xl border border-neutral-200 shadow-sm">
                            <div class="grid grid-cols-12 gap-6 items-center">
                                <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12 border-e border-neutral-200 pr-6">
                                    <label class="form-label font-bold text-primary-600 italic mb-2 block">4. Límite de Gasto Dominio</label>
                                    <div class="flex">
                                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-3 border rounded-e-0 border-e-0 rounded-s-md border-neutral-200 bg-white text-primary-600 font-bold">$</span>
                                        <input type="number" step="0.01" name="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.html_name }}" 
                                            class="form-control grow rounded-ss-none rounded-es-none" 
                                            value="{{ form.authorized_monthly_budget.value|stringformat:".2f"|default:"0.00" }}">
                                    </div>
                                </div>

                                <div class="md:col-span-6 col-span-12 pl-4">
                                    <label class="form-label font-bold text-secondary-light mb-2 block">Estado Operativo</label>
                                    <label class="inline-flex items-center cursor-pointer">
                                        <input type="checkbox" name="status" value="active" class="sr-only peer" {% if object.status == 'active' or not object %}checked{% endif %}>
                                        <span class="relative w-11 h-6 bg-gray-400 peer-focus:outline-none rounded-full peer peer-checked:after:translate-x-full peer-checked:after:border-white after:content-[''] after:absolute after:top-[2px] after:start-[2px] after:bg-white after:border-gray-300 after:border after:rounded-full after:h-5 after:w-5 after:transition-all peer-checked:bg-primary-600"></span>
                                        <span class="ms-3 font-medium peer-checked:text-primary-600 text-gray-600 text-sm">En Producción</span>
                                    </label>
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div class="col-span-12 flex items-center justify-end gap-3 mt-8 border-t pt-6">
                        <a href="{% url 'projects:list' %}" class="btn btn-outline-neutral-600 px-8 py-3 radius-8">Cancelar</a>
                        <button class="btn btn-primary-600 px-8 py-3 radius-8 flex items-center gap-2" type="submit">
                            <iconify-icon icon="solar:diskette-bold-duotone" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon>
                            {% if object %}Actualizar Datos{% else %}Crear Proyecto{% endif %}
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </form>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>

    <div class="col-span-12 lg:col-span-4">
    <div class="card border-0 shadow-sm radius-12 sticky top-24">
        <div class="card-header border-b border-neutral-200 p-6 bg-neutral-50 radius-t-12">
            <h6 class="text-base font-bold mb-0 flex items-center gap-2">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:shield-check-bold-duotone" class="text-primary-600 text-xl"></iconify-icon>
                Validación de Accesos
            </h6>
        </div>
        <div class="card-body p-6">
            <div class="space-y-6">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-4" id="row-gsc">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <iconify-icon icon="logos:google-search-console" class="text-2xl"></iconify-icon>
                        <span class="text-sm font-bold">Search Console</span>
                    </div>
                    <span class="connection-indicator {% if object.gsc_verified %}status-connected{% else %}status-disconnected{% endif %}" id="status-gsc">
                        {% if object.gsc_verified %}Conectado{% else %}Offline{% endif %}
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-4" id="row-ga4">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <iconify-icon icon="logos:google-analytics" class="text-2xl"></iconify-icon>
                        <span class="text-sm font-bold">Analytics GA4</span>
                    </div>
                    <span class="connection-indicator {% if object.ga4_verified %}status-connected{% else %}status-disconnected{% endif %}" id="status-ga4">
                        {% if object.ga4_verified %}Conectado{% else %}Offline{% endif %}
                    </span>
                </div>

                <div class="flex items-center justify-between border-b pb-4" id="row-ads">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <iconify-icon icon="logos:google-ads" class="text-2xl"></iconify-icon>
                        <span class="text-sm font-bold">Google Ads API</span>
                    </div>
                    <span class="connection-indicator {% if object.ads_verified %}status-connected{% else %}status-disconnected{% endif %}" id="status-ads">
                        {% if object.ads_verified %}Conectado{% else %}Offline{% endif %}
                    </span>
                </div>

                {% if object.pk %}
                <button type="button" id="btn-test-all" onclick="runAllTests()" class="btn btn-primary-600 w-full py-3 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-primary-200">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:play-bold-duotone" id="btn-icon" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon>
                    <span>Testear Conexiones</span>
                </button>
                {% else %}
                <div class="p-4 bg-yellow-50 border border-yellow-100 rounded-lg text-xs text-yellow-700">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:info-circle-bold" class="align-middle"></iconify-icon>
                    Guarda el proyecto primero para poder testear las APIs.
                </div>
                {% endif %}
                
                <div id="test-log" class="text-[12px] text-neutral-500 mt-2 hidden p-2 bg-neutral-100 rounded">
                    </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}

<script>
    // 1. Mensaje de control para saber si el script cargó
    console.log("🚀 Script de validación de conexiones cargado correctamente.");

    // 2. Definimos la función de forma EXPLÍCITA en el objeto window
    window.runAllTests = async function() {
        console.log("Iniciando testAllConnections...");
        
        const projectId = "{{ object.pk|default:'' }}";
        
        if (!projectId || projectId === "" || projectId === "None") {
            alert("⚠️ No se puede testear un proyecto nuevo. Guárdalo primero.");
            return;
        }

        const btn = document.getElementById('btn-test-all');
        const icon = document.getElementById('btn-icon');
        const log = document.getElementById('test-log');

        btn.disabled = true;
        if (icon) icon.setAttribute('icon', "eos-icons:loading");
        if (log) {
            log.classList.remove('hidden');
            log.innerHTML = "Iniciando validación de APIs...";
        }

        const services = [
            { id: 'gsc', label: 'Search Console' },
            { id: 'ga4', label: 'Analytics GA4' },
            { id: 'ads', label: 'Google Ads' }
        ];

        for (const service of services) {
            const statusEl = document.getElementById(`status-${service.id}`);
            if (statusEl) statusEl.innerText = "⏳ Probando...";
            
            try {
                // AQUÍ ES DONDE LLAMAMOS AL PATH DE TU URLS.PY
                // Se traduce a: /projects/1/test-access/?type=gsc
                const url = "{% url 'projects:test_access' object.pk|default:0 %}?type=" + service.id;
                console.log("Llamando a:", url);

                const response = await fetch(url);
                const data = await response.json();

                if (data.status === 'success') {
                    if (statusEl) {
                        statusEl.className = 'connection-indicator status-connected';
                        statusEl.innerText = 'Conectado';
                    }
                } else {
                    if (statusEl) {
                        statusEl.className = 'connection-indicator status-disconnected';
                        statusEl.innerText = 'Error';
                    }
                    if (log) log.innerHTML += `<br><span style="color:#ef4444">× ${service.label}:</span> ${data.message}`;
                }
            } catch (error) {
                console.error("Error en fetch:", error);
                if (statusEl) {
                    statusEl.className = 'connection-indicator status-disconnected';
                    statusEl.innerText = 'Fallo de Red';
                }
            }
        }

        btn.disabled = false;
        if (icon) icon.setAttribute('icon', "solar:play-bold-duotone");
    };
</script>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\projects\project_list.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load static %}
{% load humanize %}


{% block breadcrumb %}
<div class="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 mb-6">
    <h6 class="font-semibold mb-0 dark:text-white">
        {% if object %}List View{% else %}Listado de Proyectos{% endif %}
    </h6>
    <ul class="flex items-center gap-[6px]">
        <li class="font-medium">
            <a href="{% url 'clients:list' %}" class="flex items-center gap-2 hover:text-primary-600 dark:text-white">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:users-group-two-rounded-broken" class="icon text-lg"></iconify-icon>
                Proyectos
            </a>
        </li>
        <li class="dark:text-white">-</li>
        <li class="font-medium dark:text-white">Listado</li>
    </ul>
</div>
{% endblock %}

{% block content %}
    <div class="card h-full p-0 rounded-xl border-0 overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
        <div class="card-header border-b border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 bg-white dark:bg-neutral-700 py-4 px-6 flex items-center flex-wrap gap-3 justify-between">
            <div class="flex items-center flex-wrap gap-3">
                <form class="navbar-search flex items-center" method="GET">
                    <input type="text" class="bg-white dark:bg-neutral-700 h-10 w-auto border-neutral-200 rounded-lg" name="search" placeholder="Buscar dominio..." value="{{ request.GET.search }}">
                    <button type="submit" class="ms-[-35px] mt-1">
                        <iconify-icon icon="ion:search-outline" class="icon text-neutral-400"></iconify-icon>
                    </button>
                </form>
            </div>
            <a href="{% url 'projects:create' %}{% if current_client %}?client={{ current_client.id }}{% endif %}" class="btn btn-primary-600 text-sm btn-sm px-4 py-2 rounded-lg flex items-center gap-2 shadow-sm">
                <iconify-icon icon="ic:baseline-plus" class="icon text-xl line-height-1"></iconify-icon>
                Nuevo Proyecto
            </a>
        </div>

        <div class="card-body p-6">
            <div class="grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-2 2xl:grid-cols-3 3xl:grid-cols-4 gap-6">
                {% for project in projects %}
<div class="user-grid-card h-full">
    <div class="relative border border-neutral-200 dark:border-neutral-600 rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-white dark:bg-neutral-800 h-full flex flex-col shadow-sm hover:shadow-md transition-shadow">
        
        <div class="h-16 bg-neutral-50 dark:bg-neutral-700/20 border-b border-neutral-100 flex justify-end items-center p-2 gap-2">
            
            <a href="{% url 'projects:integrations' project.pk %}" 
               class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/90 border border-neutral-200 flex justify-center items-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-success-600 hover:border-success-200 transition-all shadow-sm"
               title="Integraciones Google">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:link-circle-linear" class="text-xl"></iconify-icon>
            </a>

            <a href="{% url 'projects:update' project.pk %}" 
               class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/90 border border-neutral-200 flex justify-center items-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-primary-600 hover:border-primary-200 transition-all shadow-sm"
               title="Ajustes del Proyecto">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:pen-new-square-linear" class="text-lg"></iconify-icon>
            </a>

            <a href="{% url 'projects:delete' project.pk %}" 
               class="w-8 h-8 rounded-lg bg-white/90 border border-neutral-200 flex justify-center items-center text-neutral-400 hover:text-danger-600 hover:border-danger-200 transition-all shadow-sm"
               title="Eliminar Proyecto">
                <iconify-icon icon="solar:trash-bin-trash-outline" class="text-lg"></iconify-icon>
            </a>
        </div>

        <div class="pe-6 pb-6 ps-6 text-center mt--40 flex-grow">
            <div class="w-16 h-16 ms-auto me-auto -mt-[32px] bg-white border-2 border-white shadow-md rounded-full overflow-hidden flex items-center justify-center relative z-10">
                <img src="https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain={{ project.domain }}&sz=128" 
                     alt="{{ project.name }}" 
                     class="w-10 h-10 object-contain"
                     onerror="this.src='https://ui-avatars.com/api/?name={{ project.name }}&background=6366f1&color=fff'">
            </div>

            <h6 class="text-lg mb-0 mt-3 truncate font-bold text-secondary-light">{{ project.name }}</h6>
            <span class="text-neutral-400 text-[11px] mb-4 block italic">{{ project.domain }}</span>
<!--AQUI ES DONDE ESTAN LOS BOTONES DE CONNECCIONES-->
            <div class="mt-6 pt-5 border-t border-neutral-100 dark:border-neutral-700">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <p class="text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-neutral-400 tracking-wider">Estado de Datos API</p>
                    
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg {% if project.gsc_verified %}bg-blue-50{% else %}bg-neutral-50{% endif %}">
                            {% if project.gsc_verified %}
                                <iconify-icon icon="logos:google-search-console" class="text-lg" title="GSC: Conectado"></iconify-icon>
                            {% else %}
                                <iconify-icon icon="simple-icons:googlesearchconsole" style="color: #cbd5e1;" class="text-lg" title="GSC: Pendiente"></iconify-icon>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>

                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg {% if project.ga4_verified %}bg-orange-50{% else %}bg-neutral-50{% endif %}">
                            {% if project.ga4_verified %}
                                <iconify-icon icon="logos:google-analytics" class="text-lg" title="GA4: Conectado"></iconify-icon>
                            {% else %}
                                <iconify-icon icon="simple-icons:googleanalytics" style="color: #cbd5e1;" class="text-lg" title="GA4: Pendiente"></iconify-icon>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>

                        <div class="flex items-center justify-center w-8 h-8 rounded-lg {% if project.ads_verified %}bg-green-50{% else %}bg-neutral-50{% endif %}">
                            {% if project.ads_verified %}
                                <iconify-icon icon="logos:google-ads" class="text-lg" title="Ads: Conectado"></iconify-icon>
                            {% else %}
                                <iconify-icon icon="simple-icons:googleads" style="color: #cbd5e1;" class="text-lg" title="Ads: Pendiente"></iconify-icon>
                            {% endif %}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="mb-4"></div>
            
            

            {% with available_perc=project.client.get_available_percentage %}
            <div class="center-border relative rounded-xl p-3 flex items-center gap-4 border transition-colors
                {% if available_perc > 50 %} bg-success-50 border-success-100
                {% elif available_perc < 30 and available_perc > 0 %} bg-warning-50 border-warning-100
                {% elif available_perc <= 0 %} bg-danger-50 border-danger-100
                {% else %} bg-neutral-50 border-neutral-100 {% endif %}">
                
                <div class="text-center w-1/2 border-e border-neutral-200/50">
                    <h6 class="text-sm mb-0 font-bold 
                        {% if available_perc > 50 %}text-success-600
                        {% elif available_perc < 30 %}text-warning-600
                        {% elif available_perc <= 0 %}text-danger-600
                        {% endif %}">
                        ${{ project.client.remaining_budget|floatformat:0 }}
                    </h6>
                    <span class="text-neutral-400 text-[9px] uppercase font-bold tracking-wider">Saldo API</span>
                </div>
                
                <div class="text-center w-1/2">
                    <h6 class="text-sm mb-0 text-secondary-light font-bold">{{ project.tracked_keywords.count|default:"0" }}</h6>
                    <span class="text-neutral-400 text-[9px] uppercase font-bold tracking-wider">Keywords</span>
                </div>
            </div>
            {% endwith %}

            <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 mt-5">
                <a href="{% url 'keyword_research:magic_tool_home' %}?project={{ project.pk }}" class="btn btn-primary-600 py-2.5 text-[11px] rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-bold shadow-sm transition-all hover:bg-primary-700">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:magic-stick-3-outline" class="text-base"></iconify-icon> Magic Tool
                </a>
                <a href="{% url 'keyword_research:keyword_magic' project.pk %}" class="btn btn-outline-neutral-600 py-2.5 text-[11px] rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center gap-2 font-bold hover:bg-neutral-50">
                    <iconify-icon icon="solar:chart-2-outline" class="text-base"></iconify-icon> Dashboard
                </a>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>  

                {% empty %}
                <div class="col-span-full py-20 text-center">
                    <div class="flex flex-col items-center">
                        <iconify-icon icon="solar:folder-error-outline" class="text-6xl text-neutral-200 mb-4"></iconify-icon>
                        <h5 class="text-neutral-500">No se encontraron proyectos</h5>
                        <p class="text-neutral-400 text-sm">Prueba con otro término o crea un nuevo dominio.</p>
                    </div>
                </div>
                {% endfor %}
            </div>

            {% if is_paginated %}
            <div class="flex items-center justify-between flex-wrap gap-2 mt-6">
                <span class="text-sm text-neutral-500">Proyectos {{ page_obj.start_index }} - {{ page_obj.end_index }}</span>
                <ul class="pagination flex items-center gap-2">
                    {% if page_obj.has_previous %}
                    <li class="page-item"><a class="page-link bg-neutral-100 p-2 rounded-lg" href="?page={{ page_obj.previous_page_number }}{% if request.GET.search %}&search={{ request.GET.search }}{% endif %}"><iconify-icon icon="ep:arrow-left"></iconify-icon></a></li>
                    {% endif %}
                    <li class="page-item"><span class="bg-primary-600 text-white px-3 py-1 rounded-lg">{{ page_obj.number }}</span></li>
                    {% if page_obj.has_next %}
                    <li class="page-item"><a class="page-link bg-neutral-100 p-2 rounded-lg" href="?page={{ page_obj.next_page_number }}{% if request.GET.search %}&search={{ request.GET.search }}{% endif %}"><iconify-icon icon="ep:arrow-right"></iconify-icon></a></li>
                    {% endif %}
                </ul>
            </div>
            {% endif %}
        </div>
    </div>

{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\projects\project_list1.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
        <h2>Proyectos SEO</h2>
        <a href="{% url 'projects:create' %}" class="btn btn-primary">+ Nuevo Proyecto</a>
    </div>
    <div class="card">
        <div class="card-body">
            <table id="projectsTable" class="table table-striped">
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Proyecto / Dominio</th>
                        <th>Cliente</th>
                        <th>Presupuesto (Propio/Heredado)</th>
                        <th>Disponible Real</th>
                        <th>Acciones</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    {% for project in projects %}
                    <tr>
                        <td>
                            <strong>{{ project.name }}</strong><br>
                            <small class="text-muted">{{ project.domain }}</small>
                        </td>
                        <td>{{ project.client.name }}</td>
                        <td>
                            {% if project.authorized_monthly_budget > 0 %}
                                ${{ project.authorized_monthly_budget }} (Fijo)
                            {% else %}
                                <span class="badge bg-light text-dark">Heredado del Cliente</span>
                            {% endif %}
                        </td>
                        <td class="fw-bold {% if project.get_available_budget < 10 %}text-warning{% endif %}">
                            ${{ project.get_available_budget|floatformat:2 }}
                        </td>
                        <td>
                            <a href="{% url 'projects:update' project.pk %}" class="btn btn-sm btn-light">Configurar APIs</a>
                            <div class="btn-group">
                                <a href="{% url 'projects:update' project.pk %}" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary">
                                    <i class="bi bi-gear"></i>
                                </a>
                                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-success" title="Test GSC/GA4">
                                    <i class="bi bi-google"></i>
                                </button>
                            </div>
                        </td>
                    </tr>
                    {% endfor %}
                </tbody>
            </table>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\registration\login.html`

```html
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="es">
<head>
    <meta charset="utf-8" />
    <title>Iniciar Sesión | SEOSuite</title>
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/css/bootstrap.min.css" rel="stylesheet" />
    <link href="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/@mdi/font@7.4.47/css/materialdesignicons.min.css" rel="stylesheet" />
    
    <style>
        body {
            background-color: #f3f4f6;
            height: 100vh;
            display: flex;
            align-items: center;
            justify-content: center;
        }
        .card-login {
            border: none;
            border-radius: 15px;
            box-shadow: 0 0.75rem 1.5rem rgba(18,38,63,.03);
        }
        .btn-primary {
            background-color: #3bafda; /* Color Hando Theme */
            border-color: #3bafda;
        }
        .btn-primary:hover {
            background-color: #3295ba;
            border-color: #3295ba;
        }
    </style>
</head>

<body>
    <div class="container">
        <div class="row justify-content-center">
            <div class="col-md-8 col-lg-6 col-xl-4">
                <div class="card card-login">
                    <div class="card-body p-4">
                        
                        <div class="text-center w-75 m-auto mb-4">
                            <h2 class="text-dark text-center mt-0 fw-bold">SEOSuite</h2>
                            <p class="text-muted mb-4">Ingresa tus credenciales para acceder al OS.</p>
                        </div>

                        <form action="{% url 'login' %}" method="POST">
                            {% csrf_token %}

                            {% if form.errors %}
                                <div class="alert alert-danger" role="alert">
                                    <i class="mdi mdi-block-helper me-2"></i> Usuario o contraseña incorrectos.
                                </div>
                            {% endif %}

                            <div class="mb-3">
                                <label for="id_username" class="form-label">Usuario</label>
                                <input class="form-control" type="text" id="id_username" name="username" required="" placeholder="Ingresa tu usuario" autofocus>
                            </div>

                            <div class="mb-3">
                                <label for="id_password" class="form-label">Contraseña</label>
                                <div class="input-group input-group-merge">
                                    <input type="password" id="id_password" name="password" class="form-control" placeholder="Ingresa tu contraseña">
                                    <div class="input-group-text" data-password="false">
                                        <span class="password-eye"></span>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="mb-3">
                                <div class="form-check">
                                    <input type="checkbox" class="form-check-input" id="checkbox-signin" checked>
                                    <label class="form-check-label" for="checkbox-signin">Recordarme</label>
                                </div>
                            </div>

                            <div class="text-center d-grid">
                                <button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit"> Iniciar Sesión </button>
                            </div>

                        </form>

                        <div class="text-center mt-3">
                            <p class="text-muted"><small>¿Olvidaste tu contraseña? Contacta al Admin.</small></p>
                        </div>

                    </div> </div> </div> </div> </div> <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
</body>
</html>
```

```html
                        <i data-feather="zap" class="text-warning icon-lg"></i>

</div>


{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\seo\keyword_magic.html`

```html
{% extends 'layouts/base.html' %}
{% load static %}
{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row">
        <div class="col-12">
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <h4 class="header-title">Keyword Magic Tool: {{ project.name }}</h4>
                    <p class="text-muted">Sube tu exportación de Google Ads (CSV) para analizar volúmenes.</p>
                    
                    <form method="POST" enctype="multipart/form-data" class="mb-4">
                        {% csrf_token %}
                        <div class="input-group">
                            <input type="file" name="csv_file" class="form-control" accept=".csv">
                            <button class="btn btn-primary" type="submit">Importar Keywords</button>
                        </div>
                    </form>

                    <table class="table table-hover mb-0">
                        <thead>
                            <tr>
                                <th>Keyword</th>
                                <th>Volumen Mensual</th>
                                <th>Competencia</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            {% for kw in keywords %}
                            <tr>
                                <td><strong>{{ kw.keyword }}</strong></td>
                                <td>{{ kw.avg_monthly_searches|default:"0" }}</td>
                                <td><span class="badge bg-soft-info text-info">{{ kw.competition_level }}</span></td>
                            </tr>
                            {% empty %}
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="3" class="text-center">No hay datos. Sube un CSV para empezar.</td>
                            </tr>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
{% endblock %}
```

## `templates\seo\url_audit.html`

```html
{% extends 'base.html' %}
{% block content %}
<div class="container-fluid">
    <div class="row">
        <div class="col-12">
            <div class="card">
                <div class="card-body">
                    <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-4">
                        <h4 class="header-title">Auditoría de Indexación: {{ project.name }}</h4>
                        <form method="POST">
                            {% csrf_token %}
                            <button type="submit" class="btn btn-success">
                                <i data-feather="refresh-cw" class="icon-xs me-1"></i> Escanear Sitemap
                            </button>
                            <a href="#" 
                                onclick="iniciarInspeccionMasiva(event, this.href)"
                                class="btn btn-primary shadow-sm">
                                <i data-feather="zoom-in" class="icon-xs me-1"></i> Inspección Masiva (API)
                            </a>
                        </form>
                    </div>
                    <table class="table table-centered table-nowrap mb-0">
                        <thead class="table-light">
                            <tr>
                                <th>Página (URL)</th>
                                <th>Estado en Google</th>

                                <th>Google Referencia</th>
                                <th>Último Rastreo</th>
                                <th>Acción</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody>
                            {% for audit in audits %}
                            <tr>
                                <td>
                                    <a href="{{ audit.url }}" target="_blank" class="text-body fw-bold">{{ audit.url }}</a>
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    {% if audit.status == 'Submitted and indexed' %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-soft-success text-success fs-12">Indexada</span>
                                    {% elif audit.status == 'Solicitud de Indexación Enviada' %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-soft-info text-info fs-12">Solicitada ⚡</span>
                                    {% elif audit.status == 'URL is unknown to Google' %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-soft-warning text-warning fs-12">No Descubierta</span>
                                    {% else %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-soft-danger text-danger fs-12">{{ audit.verdict|default:"Pendiente" }}</span>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </td>
                                 <td class="align-middle">
                                    {% if audit.sitemap_status == "Success" %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-success">Válido</span>
                                    {% elif audit.sitemap_status == "No se pudo obtener" %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-danger">Error de Lectura</span>
                                        <i class="mdi mdi-help-circle text-muted" title="Google no pudo acceder al archivo o la URL no es un XML"></i>
                                    {% else %}
                                        <span class="badge bg-light text-dark">Pendiente</span>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    <small class="text-muted">{{ audit.status|default:"Pendiente de inspección" }}</small>
                                </td>
                                <td>
                                    <a href="{% url 'serp:inspect_url' project_pk=project.pk pk=audit.id %}" class="btn btn-sm btn-light">
                                        <i data-feather="search" class="icon-xs"></i>
                                    </a>
                                    {% if audit.status != 'Submitted and indexed' %}
                                        <a href="{% url 'serp:request_indexing' pk=project.pk audit_id=audit.id %}" 
                                        class="btn btn-sm btn-soft-warning" 
                                        title="Forzar Indexación">
                                            <i data-feather="zap" class="icon-xs text-warning"></i>
                                        </a>
                                    {% endif %}
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            {% empty %}
                            <tr>
                                <td colspan="4" class="text-center">No hay URLs auditadas. ¡Empecemos ahora!</td>
                            </tr>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>
    </div>
</div>
    {% block scripts %}
    <script>
        // Usamos una función para envolver todo y asegurar que SweetAlert esté cargado
        function iniciarInspeccionMasiva(e, url) {
            e.preventDefault();
            
            Swal.fire({
                title: 'Inspeccionando URLs...',
                html: `
                    <div class="mt-3">
                        <p>Estamos consultando la API de Google Search Console.</p>
                        <p class="text-muted small">Esto puede tardar unos minutos dependiendo de la cantidad de páginas.</p>
                    </div>
                `,
                allowOutsideClick: false,
                allowEscapeKey: false,
                showConfirmButton: false,
                didOpen: () => {
                    Swal.showLoading();
                }
            });

            // Redirigir después de mostrar el popup
            window.location.href = url;
        }

        // Esperar a que el DOM esté listo
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
            // Inicializar iconos de nuevo por si acaso
            if (typeof feather !== 'undefined') {
                feather.replace();
            }
        });
    </script>
    {% endblock scripts %}
{% endblock %}
```

---

## `templates\serp\serp_analysis.html`

```html
{% extends "base.html" %}
{% load humanize %}

{% block content %}
<div class="page-content">
    <div class="container-fluid">

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-12">
                <div class="page-title-box d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">
                    <div>
                        <h4 class="page-title">Analytics: <span class="text-primary">{{ keyword_name }}</span></h4>
                        <ol class="breadcrumb m-0 fs-12">
                            <li class="breadcrumb-item"><a href="javascript: void(0);">Investigación</a></li>
                            <li class="breadcrumb-item active">Análisis de SERP</li>
                        </ol>
                    </div>
                    
                    <div class="page-title-right">
                        <a href="javascript:history.back()" class="btn btn-white btn-sm rounded-pill shadow-sm px-3 border">
                            <i class="mdi mdi-arrow-left me-1 text-primary"></i> 
                            <span class="fw-semibold">Volver al Magic Tool</span>
                        </a>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="row">
            <div class="col-md-6 col-xl-4">
                <div class="card h-100 shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <div class="d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">
                            <div>
                                <p class="text-muted text-uppercase fw-semibold fs-13 mb-2">Volumen de Búsqueda</p>
                                <h3 class="mb-0 text-dark">{{ volume|intcomma }}</h3>
                            </div>
                            <div class="avatar-sm bg-soft-primary rounded">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-trending-up avatar-title font-22 text-primary"></i>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="mt-3">
                            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-1">
                                <span class="text-muted fs-12"><i class="mdi mdi-flag-variant text-danger me-1"></i>{{ country_name }}</span>
                                <span class="text-dark fw-bold">100%</span>
                            </div>
                            <div class="progress progress-sm" style="height: 5px;">
                                <div class="progress-bar bg-primary" role="progressbar" style="width: 100%"></div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-6 col-xl-4">
                <div class="card h-100 shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <div class="d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">
                            <div>
                                <p class="text-muted text-uppercase fw-semibold fs-13 mb-2">Keyword Difficulty</p>
                                <h3 class="mb-0">{{ kd }}%</h3>
                            </div>
                            <div class="avatar-sm {% if kd > 60 %}bg-soft-danger{% else %}bg-soft-success{% endif %} rounded">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-gauge avatar-title font-22 {% if kd > 60 %}text-danger{% else %}text-success{% endif %}"></i>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="mt-3">
                            {% if kd > 70 %}<span class="badge bg-soft-danger text-danger">Muy Difícil</span>
                            {% elif kd > 40 %}<span class="badge bg-soft-warning text-warning">Media</span>
                            {% else %}<span class="badge bg-soft-success text-success">Fácil</span>{% endif %}
                            <span class="text-muted fs-12 ms-2">Basado en competidores</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-md-6 col-xl-4">
                <div class="card h-100 shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <div class="d-flex align-items-center justify-content-between">
                            <div>
                                <p class="text-muted text-uppercase fw-semibold fs-13 mb-2">CPC Promedio</p>
                                <h3 class="mb-0">${{ cpc|floatformat:2 }}</h3>
                            </div>
                            <div class="avatar-sm bg-soft-info rounded">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-currency-usd avatar-title font-22 text-info"></i>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="mt-3">
                            <span class="badge bg-soft-info text-info rounded-pill px-3">Informativa</span>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="row mt-4">
            <div class="col-12">
                <div class="card shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-header bg-white py-3">
                        <h5 class="card-title mb-0"><i class="mdi mdi-google me-2 text-primary"></i>Resultados en Tiempo Real de Google</h5>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card-body">
                        <table id="datatable-serp" class="table table-centered table-nowrap mb-0 w-100">
                            <thead class="bg-light">
                                <tr>
                                    <th class="text-center" style="width: 60px;">Pos.</th>
                                    <th>Detalles del Resultado Orgánico</th>
                                    <th>Dominio</th>
                                </tr>
                            </thead>
                            <tbody>
                                {% for res in organic_results %}
                                    {% if res.type == 'organic' %}
                                    <tr>
                                        <td class="text-center fw-bold text-muted">{{ forloop.counter }}</td>
                                        <td class="py-3">
                                            <div class="d-flex flex-column">
                                                <a href="{% if res.url %}{{ res.url }}{% elif res.link %}{{ res.link }}{% else %}#{% endif %}" target="_blank" class="fw-bold text-primary fs-15 text-decoration-none mb-1">
                                                    {{ res.title|default:"Sin título" }}
                                                </a>
                                                <p class="text-muted small mb-0 fs-12 lh-sm">{{ res.description|default:""|truncatechars:120 }}</p>
                                            </div>
                                        </td>
                                        <td>
                                            <span class="text-success fw-medium small">
                                                <i class="mdi mdi-link-variant me-1"></i>{% if res.domain %}{{ res.domain }}{% else %}google.com{% endif %}
                                            </span>
                                        </td>
                                    </tr>
                                    {% endif %}
                                {% endfor %}
                            </tbody>
                        </table>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <div class="row mt-4 pb-5">
            <div class="col-xl-4 col-md-6 mb-4">
                <div class="card h-100 shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-header bg-white py-3 border-bottom d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                        <h6 class="card-title mb-0 fw-bold">Keyword Ideas</h6>
                        <span class="badge bg-soft-warning text-warning fs-10">DATABASE</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card-body p-0">
                        <div class="list-group list-group-flush">
                            {% for idea in db_ideas %}
                            <div class="list-group-item d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center border-0 py-2 fs-13">
                                <span class="text-dark">{{ idea.keyword }}</span>
                                <span class="text-muted fw-bold">{{ idea.search_volume|intcomma }}</span>
                            </div>
                            {% empty %}
                            <div class="p-4 text-center text-muted fs-12">No hay ideas similares.</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-xl-4 col-md-6 mb-4">
                <div class="card h-100 shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-header bg-white py-3 border-bottom d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                        <h6 class="card-title mb-0 fw-bold">Questions</h6>
                        <span class="badge bg-soft-primary text-primary fs-10">GOOGLE LIVE</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card-body p-0">
                        <div class="list-group list-group-flush">
                            {% for q in google_questions %}
                            <div class="list-group-item border-0 py-2 fs-13 text-dark">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-comment-question-outline text-muted me-2"></i> {{ q.question|default:q.title }}
                            </div>
                            {% empty %}
                            <div class="p-4 text-center text-muted fs-12">Sin preguntas detectadas.</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="col-xl-4 col-md-12 mb-4">
                <div class="card h-100 shadow-sm border-0">
                    <div class="card-header bg-white py-3 border-bottom d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center">
                        <h6 class="card-title mb-0 fw-bold">Related Searches</h6>
                        <span class="badge bg-soft-success text-success fs-10">GOOGLE LIVE</span>
                    </div>
                    <div class="card-body p-0">
                        <div class="list-group list-group-flush">
                            {% for rel in google_related %}
                            <a href="#" class="list-group-item list-group-item-action border-0 py-2 fs-13 text-dark">
                                <i class="mdi mdi-magnify text-muted me-2"></i> {{ rel.text|default:rel.title }}
                            </a>
                            {% empty %}
                            <div class="p-4 text-center text-muted fs-12">Sin búsquedas relacionadas.</div>
                            {% endfor %}
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

    </div> 
</div>

<style>
    .bg-soft-primary { background-color: rgba(114, 124, 245, 0.1) !important; }
    .bg-soft-success { background-color: rgba(10, 207, 151, 0.1) !important; }
    .bg-soft-danger { background-color: rgba(250, 92, 124, 0.1) !important; }
    .bg-soft-warning { background-color: rgba(255, 188, 0, 0.1) !important; }
    .bg-soft-info { background-color: rgba(57, 175, 209, 0.1) !important; }
    .fs-13 { font-size: 13px !important; }
    .fs-15 { font-size: 15px !important; }
    .fs-12 { font-size: 12px !important; }
    .fs-10 { font-size: 10px !important; }
</style>

{% endblock %}

{% block extra_js %}
<script>
$(document).ready(function() {
    // Inicialización del DataTable SERP
    if ($.fn.DataTable.isDataTable('#datatable-serp')) {
        $('#datatable-serp').DataTable().destroy();
    }

    $('#datatable-serp').DataTable({
        "language": { "url": "//cdn.datatables.net/plug-ins/1.13.7/i18n/es-ES.json" },
        "pageLength": 10,
        "order": [[0, "asc"]],
        "dom": '<"d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3"f>rtip',
    });
});
</script>
{% endblock %}
```
